        OR = {

                culture.dialect = dialect:angevin_dialect
            }
        }
    }

    capital_trigger = {
        province_definition = province_definition:akan_province

    }

        OR = {
            culture.dialect = dialect:angevin_dialect
            overlord ?= {
                culture.dialect = dialect:angevin_dialect
            }
        }
    }

    capital_trigger = {
        province_definition = province_definition:jujuy_province
    }

    location_trigger = {
    }
}

    capital_trigger = {
        province_definition = province_definition:reao_province
    }

    location_trigger = {
    }
}

    country_trigger = {
        OR = {
            culture.dialect = dialect:angevin_dialect
            overlord ?= {
                culture.dialect = dialect:angevin_dialect
            }
        }
    }
